# Combined Roblox Codes Articles

Source docs:
- [Beebom Roblox Codes articles](./Beebom%20Roblox%20Codes%20articles.md)
- [roblox Den Roblox Codes Articles](./roblox%20Den%20Roblox%20Codes%20Articles.md)

Merge policy:
- High-confidence matches were auto-merged.
- Your manual decisions from the previous review were applied.
- Remaining former confusion items were left as separate rows and not merged.
- One Beebom tag page was excluded from the combined list because it was not a game code article.
- One RobloxDen row had an invalid title, so the game name was derived from its slug.

Total combined rows: 3921
Rows with both sources: 576
Manual merges applied: 16
Auto merges kept: 560
Beebom-only rows: 136
RobloxDen-only rows: 3209
